一种新型冻干减毒麻疹–麻疹(MR)疫苗制剂的开发,旨在实现非洲低成本生产

Development of a New Lyophilized Live-Attenuated Measles–Rubella (MR) Vaccine Formulation Designed for Lower-Cost Production in Africa
作者： Michael Z. Wang, Prashant Kumar, Kathryn Secrist, Soraia Saleh-Birdjandi, Sagar Salave, Ranjeet Desai, Ozan S. Kumru, Sangeeta B. Joshi, David B. Volkin
摘要中文翻译背景/目标:本研究描述了一种新型、冻干、减毒麻疹–麻疹(MR)疫苗制剂的开发,旨在实现非洲低成本生产。该制剂由稳定剂溶液(仅含非动物来源的赋形剂)和高滴度的MR病毒制剂(通过现代化工艺在MRC-5细胞中生产)组成,比例分别为95%和5%(体积比)。方法:开发了实验室规模和中试规模的冻干循环,实施了高通量RT-qPCR为基础的病毒感染力检测,并采用了有效的赋形剂筛选条件以确定MR病毒的稳定添加剂。基于筛选结果,设计、制备并评估了候选冻干MR制剂,通过比较储存稳定性评估。结果:首先,避免使用基于蛋白质的填充剂(许多商业冻干MR制剂中使用),而采用蔗糖/甘露醇混合物。其次,筛选了约50种赋形剂,并确定了两种额外的关键稳定成分(此前均被报道可稳定MR病毒):磷酸钠缓冲液和尿素。随后,对候选冻干MR制剂的不同变种进行了稳定性评估,以阐明蔗糖–甘露醇比例、冻干块完整性与MR病毒稳定性之间的相互关系。最终确定了优化后的MR 疫苗制剂配方(复溶后):5%(重量/体积)甘露醇、5%(重量/体积)蔗糖、50 mM尿素、25 mM磷酸钠缓冲液(pH 6.5)以及5%(体积/体积)病毒制剂。当该制剂以单剂量形式在实验室规模冻干时,两种MR病毒在实时和加速储存条件下均表现出良好的稳定性。随后,在中试规模冻干机中评估了五剂量形式,采用2倍浓度的制剂填充到更大的安瓿中,冻干后复溶至1倍浓度。结论:结果表明,一种新型、低成本、冻干、多剂量的MR制剂在非洲本地生产减毒MR 疫苗候选疫苗的潜在应用中是可行的。研究结果可为开发更经济且稳定的MR 疫苗提供依据,从而改善非洲的免疫接种计划和疾病预防工作。局限性与证据强度:本研究仅基于摘要证据,结果基于受控条件下的稳定性评估,可能无法完全反映现实世界的情况。证据强度为中等。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在开发一种新型、冻干、减毒麻疹–麻疹(MR)疫苗制剂,以实现非洲低成本生产,解决资源匮乏地区对经济且稳定的疫苗的需求。
设计与对象
本研究涉及一种冻干MR 疫苗制剂的实验室规模和中试规模冻干循环的开发,研究对象包括在MRC-5细胞中生产的病毒制剂,并在不同条件下评估其稳定性。
核心方法
开发了实验室规模和中试规模的冻干循环,实施了高通量RT-qPCR为基础的病毒感染力检测,并采用了有效的赋形剂筛选条件以确定MR病毒的稳定添加剂。
主要结果
首先,避免使用基于蛋白质的填充剂(许多商业冻干MR制剂中使用),而采用蔗糖/甘露醇混合物。其次,筛选了约50种赋形剂,并确定了两种额外的关键稳定成分:磷酸钠缓冲液和尿素。对候选冻干MR制剂的不同变种进行了稳定性评估,以阐明蔗糖–甘露醇比例、冻干块完整性与MR病毒稳定性之间的相互关系。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,一种新型、低成本、冻干、多剂量的MR制剂在非洲本地生产减毒MR 疫苗候选疫苗的潜在应用中是可行的。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发更经济且稳定的MR 疫苗提供依据,从而改善非洲的免疫接种计划和疾病预防工作。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅基于摘要证据,结果基于受控条件下的稳定性评估,可能无法完全反映现实世界的情况。证据强度为中等。

Development of a New Lyophilized Live-Attenuated Measles–Rubella (MR) Vaccine Formulation Designed for Lower-Cost Production in Africa

Authors: Michael Z. Wang, Prashant Kumar, Kathryn Secrist, Soraia Saleh-Birdjandi, Sagar Salave, Ranjeet Desai, Ozan S. Kumru, Sangeeta B. Joshi, David B. Volkin
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: This work describes the development of a new, lyophilized, live-attenuated measles–rubella (MR) vaccine formulation designed for lower-cost production in Africa. The formulation consists of a stabilizer solution (containing only non-animal-derived excipients) and high-titer MR viral bulks (produced in MRC-5 cells by a modernized process) prepared at a ratio of 95% and 5% (v/v), respectively. Methods: Both lab-scale and pilot-scale freeze-drying cycles were developed, a high-throughput RT-qPCR-based viral infectivity assay was implemented, and effective excipient screening conditions to identify stabilizing additives for MR viruses were employed. Based on the down-selected hits, candidate lyophilized MR formulations were designed, prepared, and evaluated by comparative storage stability assessments. Results: First, protein-based bulking agents (used in many commercial lyophilized MR-containing vaccines) were avoided in favor of sucrose/mannitol mixtures. Second, ~50 excipients were screened, and two additional key stabilizing components were identified (both previously reported to stabilize MR viruses): sodium phosphate buffer and urea. Next, stability assessments were performed on different variations of a candidate lyophilized MR formulation to elucidate the inter-relationships between sucrose–mannitol ratios, cake integrity, and MR virus stability. An optimized MR vaccine formulation composition (post-reconstitution) of 5% (w/v) mannitol, 5% (w/v) sucrose, 50 mM urea, 25 mM sodium phosphate buffer (pH 6.5), and 5% (v/v) viral bulks was established. When this formulation was lyophilized at lab-scale in a single-dose format, both MR viruses displayed promising stability profiles during storage under real-time and accelerated conditions. A five-dose format was then evaluated in a pilot-scale freeze-dryer by using a 2X formulation filled into larger vials, lyophilized, and then reconstituted to 1X concentration. Conclusions: Results demonstrated the feasibility of a new, lower-cost, lyophilized, multidose MR formulation for potential use with a live-attenuated MR vaccine candidate targeted for local production in Africa.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This work aimed to develop a new, lyophilized, live-attenuated measles–rubella (MR) vaccine formulation designed for lower-cost production in Africa, addressing the need for affordable and stable vaccines in resource-limited settings.
Design and population
The study involved the development of lab-scale and pilot-scale freeze-drying cycles for a lyophilized MR vaccine formulation, with the population consisting of viral bulks produced in MRC-5 cells and evaluated for stability under various conditions.
Core methods
Both lab-scale and pilot-scale freeze-drying cycles were developed, a high-throughput RT-qPCR-based viral infectivity assay was implemented, and effective excipient screening conditions were employed to identify stabilizing additives for MR viruses.
Main results
First, protein-based bulking agents were avoided in favor of sucrose/mannitol mixtures. Second, ~50 excipients were screened, and two additional key stabilizing components were identified: sodium phosphate buffer and urea. Stability assessments were performed on different variations of a candidate lyophilized MR formulation to elucidate the inter-relationships between sucrose–mannitol ratios, cake integrity, and MR virus stability.
Interpretation and novelty
The results demonstrated the feasibility of a new, lower-cost, lyophilized, multidose MR formulation for potential use with a live-attenuated MR vaccine candidate targeted for local production in Africa.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more affordable and stable MR vaccines for use in Africa, potentially improving immunization programs and disease prevention efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the results are based on stability assessments under controlled conditions, which may not fully represent real-world scenarios. The evidence strength is moderate.

东南亚5岁以下儿童疫苗接种、发病率、暴发和麻疹未完成情况的社会经济决定因素:系统综述

Socioeconomic Determinants of Measles Incidence, Outbreaks, and Vaccination Non-Completion Among Children Under Five in Southeast Asia: A Systematic Review
作者： NIK FARESHA SOFEA BINTI FARIQ IDZHAR, NURUL HUDA BINTI MISBAN, NUR HANIE INSYIRAH BINTI NORHISHAM, NAZZRA AZERA BINTI MAT ZAINI, SYARIFAH NURUL BALQIS SYED SAFARUDDIN, Zulkifli Abdul Hadi
摘要中文翻译本系统综述探讨了社会经济地位(SES)与东南亚5岁以下儿童麻疹 发病率、暴发、传播以及常规免疫接种未完成之间的关系。尽管全球文献记录了儿童疫苗接种的一般差异,但关于特定情境下的父母因素——特别是家庭收入、父母教育水平、母亲识字率和就业状况——如何与结构性医疗保健获取相互作用以驱动儿童疾病易感性的研究仍存在重大空白。通过系统地综合2016年至2025年间发表的英文实证定量观察性研究(横断面、队列研究、病例对照和生态研究),本项目旨在识别影响东南亚国家(如马来西亚、印度尼西亚、泰国、菲律宾和越南)中麻疹感染和不完全疫苗接种的主要社会经济决定因素。最终,预期结果将产生实证且情境特定的证据,以帮助公共卫生政策制定者设计有针对性的公平干预措施,加强常规免疫接种覆盖率,并预防麻疹 暴发在易感儿童群体中的传播。

问题与背景
本系统综述探讨了社会经济地位(SES)与东南亚5岁以下儿童麻疹 发病率、暴发、传播以及常规免疫接种未完成之间的关系。
设计与对象

核心方法
研究设计与人群包括2016年至2025年间发表的英文横断面、队列研究、病例对照和生态研究,关注实证定量观察性数据。
主要结果
提供的证据未报告具体结果或统计发现。

局限与证据强度
提供的证据未报告具体限制或证据强度评估。

Socioeconomic Determinants of Measles Incidence, Outbreaks, and Vaccination Non-Completion Among Children Under Five in Southeast Asia: A Systematic Review

Authors: NIK FARESHA SOFEA BINTI FARIQ IDZHAR, NURUL HUDA BINTI MISBAN, NUR HANIE INSYIRAH BINTI NORHISHAM, NAZZRA AZERA BINTI MAT ZAINI, SYARIFAH NURUL BALQIS SYED SAFARUDDIN, Zulkifli Abdul Hadi
Original abstract (English)
This systematic review investigates the relationship between socioeconomic status (SES) and measles incidence, outbreaks, transmission, and routine immunization non-completion among children under five years of age in Southeast Asia. While global literature documents general disparities in childhood vaccination, significant research gaps remain regarding how context-specific parental factors—specifically household income, parental education, maternal literacy, and employment status—interact with structural healthcare access to drive pediatric disease susceptibility. By systematically synthesizing empirical quantitative observational studies (cross-sectional, cohort, case-control, and ecological) published in English between 2016 and 2025, this project aims to isolate the primary socioeconomic determinants influencing measles infection and incomplete vaccination within Southeast Asian countries like Malaysia, Indonesia, Thailand, the Philippines, and Vietnam. Ultimately, the expected outcomes will yield empirical, context-specific evidence to help public health policymakers design targeted equity interventions, strengthen routine immunization coverage, and prevent measles outbreaks across vulnerable pediatric populations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This systematic review investigates the relationship between socioeconomic status (SES) and measles incidence, outbreaks, transmission, and routine immunization non-completion among children under five years of age in Southeast Asia.
Design and population
By systematically synthesizing empirical quantitative observational studies (cross-sectional, cohort, case-control, and ecological) published in English between 2016 and 2025, this project aims to isolate the primary socioeconomic determinants influencing measles infection and incomplete vaccination within Southeast Asian countries like Malaysia, Indonesia, Thailand, the Philippines, and Vietnam.
Core methods
The study design_population includes cross-sectional, cohort, case-control, and ecological studies published in English between 2016 and 2025, focusing on empirical quantitative observational data.
Main results
The supplied evidence does not report specific results or statistical findings.
Interpretation and novelty
The supplied evidence does not report explicit interpretation or novelty claims.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study aims to provide empirical, context-specific evidence to help public health policymakers design targeted equity interventions, strengthen routine immunization coverage, and prevent measles outbreaks across vulnerable pediatric populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or evidence strength assessment.

与住院时间及临床特征相关的因素:住院治疗的儿童麻疹病例

Factors Associated with Length of Hospital Stay and Clinical Characteristics of Children Hospitalized with Measles
作者： Leyla Beşel, Adem Karbuz, Irmak Emre, Didem Kızmaz İşançlı, Çiğdem Kırmacı, Önder Kılıçaslan, Zeynep Kılıç Dağtekin, Vildan Şahin, Erdem Öksüzoğlu, Zeynep Kılıç
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
评估住院治疗的麻疹患儿的临床特征、并发症及与住院时间相关的因素。
设计与对象
本研究为回顾性观察性研究,纳入了2023年1月至12月期间在土耳其伊斯坦布尔一家三级医疗机构住院治疗的0-18岁麻疹患儿。
核心方法
分析了人口学、临床、实验室及住院数据。
主要结果
共纳入61例住院治疗的麻疹患儿;其中52.5%年龄小于5岁,47.5%年龄大于5岁,59%为土耳其公民,41%为外国国籍,57.4%为男性,77%未接种疫苗。所有患者均出现发热和皮疹。最常见的并发症为肺炎(68.9%),其次是肝炎(11.5%)、牙龈口炎(11.5%)、眼部并发症(9.8%)和中耳炎(4.9%)。所有肝炎病例均发生于年龄大于5岁的患儿中。3例患儿(5.2%)需要儿科重症监护。
解释与创新
In univariable linear regression analyses, pneumonia/bronchopneumonia, C-reactive protein (CRP), and white blood cell count were significantly associated with longer hospital stay. In the primary multivariable linear regression model, only CRP remained independently associated with prolonged hospitalization (B = 0.017, 95% CI = 0.0002-0.0333; P = .047). Inflammatory markers were associated with prolonged hospitalization, suggesting that systemic inflammation may contribute to longer inpatient care.
科研与公卫意义
麻疹仍然是一个重要的公共卫生问题,尤其是在未接种疫苗的儿童中。需要更大规模的多中心研究来验证这些发现。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其回顾性设计,需要更大规模的多中心研究来验证这些发现。样本量相对较小,且研究仅在单一中心进行,这可能限制其结果的普遍适用性。

Factors Associated with Length of Hospital Stay and Clinical Characteristics of Children Hospitalized with Measles

Authors: Leyla Beşel, Adem Karbuz, Irmak Emre, Didem Kızmaz İşançlı, Çiğdem Kırmacı, Önder Kılıçaslan, Zeynep Kılıç Dağtekin, Vildan Şahin, Erdem Öksüzoğlu, Zeynep Kılıç
Original abstract (English)
Objective: To evaluate the clinical characteristics, complications, and factors associated with the length of hospital stay in children hospitalized with measles. Materials and Methods: This retrospective, observational study included children aged 0-18 years hospitalized with measles between January and December 2023 at a tertiary care center in Istanbul, Türkiye. Demographic, clinical, laboratory, and hospitalization data were analyzed. Results: Sixty-one hospitalized children with measles were included; 32 (52.5%) were younger than 5 years and 29 (47.5%) were older than 5 years. Fifty-nine percent were Turkish citizens, 41% were foreign nationals, and 57.4% were male. Overall, 77% were unvaccinated. Fever and rash were present in all patients. Pneumonia was the most common complication (68.9%), followed by hepatitis (11.5%), gingivostomatitis (11.5%), ocular complications (9.8%), and otitis (4.9%). All hepatitis cases occurred in children older than 5 years. Three patients (5.2%) required pediatric intensive care. In univariable linear regression analyses, pneumonia/bronchopneumonia, C-reactive protein (CRP), and white blood cell count were significantly associated with longer hospital stay. In the primary multivariable linear regression model, only CRP remained independently associated with prolonged hospitalization (B = 0.017, 95% CI = 0.0002-0.0333; P = .047). No measles-related deaths occurred. Conclusion: Measles remains a major public health problem, particularly among unvaccinated children. Inflammatory markers were associated with prolonged hospitalization, suggesting that systemic inflammation may contribute to longer inpatient care. Larger multicenter studies are needed to confirm these findings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
To evaluate the clinical characteristics, complications, and factors associated with the length of hospital stay in children hospitalized with measles.
Design and population
This retrospective, observational study included children aged 0-18 years hospitalized with measles between January and December 2023 at a tertiary care center in Istanbul, Türkiye.
Core methods
Demographic, clinical, laboratory, and hospitalization data were analyzed.
Main results
Sixty-one hospitalized children with measles were included; 52.5% were younger than 5 years, 47.5% were older than 5 years, 59% were Turkish citizens, 41% were foreign nationals, 57.4% were male, and 77% were unvaccinated. Fever and rash were present in all patients. Pneumonia was the most common complication (68.9%), followed by hepatitis (11.5%), gingivostomatitis (11.5%), ocular complications (9.8%), and otitis (4.9%). All hepatitis cases occurred in children older than 5 years. Three patients (5.2%) required pediatric intensive care.
Interpretation and novelty
In univariable linear regression analyses, pneumonia/bronchopneumonia, C-reactive protein (CRP), and white blood cell count were significantly associated with longer hospital stay. In the primary multivariable linear regression model, only CRP remained independently associated with prolonged hospitalization (B = 0.017, 95% CI = 0.0002-0.0333; P = .047). Inflammatory markers were associated with prolonged hospitalization, suggesting that systemic inflammation may contribute to longer inpatient care.
Scientific and public-health significance
Measles remains a major public health problem, particularly among unvaccinated children. Larger multicenter studies are needed to confirm these findings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and the need for larger multicenter studies to confirm these findings. The sample size is relatively small, and the study was conducted at a single center, which may limit generalizability.

从日本公开的监测数据中通过定量偏倚分析估计麻疹 疫苗效果:研究方案和统计分析计划(版本2.4)

Estimating measles vaccine effectiveness from Japanese public surveillance data with quantitative bias analysis: study protocol and statistical analysis plan (version 2.4)
作者： Satoshi Kutsuna
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在利用日本公开的监测数据,估计麻疹 疫苗的效果(VE),解决在消除背景下准确评估VE的挑战,其中病例数量较少且参考人群可能与病例产生人群不一致。
设计与对象
本研究是一项利用日本公开的监测数据估计VE的方案,重点关注麻疹病例和疫苗接种历史,涵盖三个时间段(2008-2014年、2015-2019年、2020-2025年),主要针对2-4岁儿童。
核心方法
该研究采用筛选方法,从已发表的日本汇总数据中估计VE,将一个或多个剂量的麻疹与未接种的疫苗进行比较,使用带有日历年调整的两组二项式似然模型和95百分比轮廓似然区间。
主要结果
主要结果包括2-4岁儿童的VE估计,5-19岁年龄段的VE被报告为次要结果。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为强调在VE估计中条件推断的局限性,指出敏感性范围跨越零并不意味着疫苗无效,而是反映了方法上的限制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为监测策略提供信息,并有助于理解消除背景下VE的评估,从而提高对疫苗表现和数据收集潜在偏倚的认识。
局限与证据强度
本研究承认了一些局限性,如疫苗接种数据的缺失和潜在的选择偏差,但由于使用了合成数据验证和注册的解释规则,证据强度被认为是中等的。

Estimating measles vaccine effectiveness from Japanese public surveillance data with quantitative bias analysis: study protocol and statistical analysis plan (version 2.4)

Authors: Satoshi Kutsuna
Original abstract (English)
Japan began national measles case-based surveillance in 2008 and was verified as having eliminated endemic transmission in 2015, yet importation-related clusters continue and global resurgence has renewed attention. In an elimination setting, vaccine effectiveness estimation is limited both by small case numbers and by mismatch between where cases arise and whom the reference survey represents. This study estimates VE by the screening method from published Japanese aggregate data, contrasting one or more doses against none, primarily in children aged 2-4 across three periods (2008-2014, 2015-2019, 2020-2025), with calendar year adjusted within period by a two-group binomial likelihood and 95 percent profile-likelihood intervals. Ages 5-19 are reported as important secondary results. The distinguishing feature is that quantitative bias analysis is treated as a co-equal part of the result rather than an appendix: missingness in vaccination history is modelled explicitly through the completed vaccination share, with the share among those of unknown status set both as a MAR-equivalent state and at fixed absolute values; selection mismatch between the reference sample and the case-generating population enters as an independently fixed multiplier K; and residual confounding together with differential case ascertainment enters as a multiplier B for the extension to clinical VE. Case counts by age and vaccination history exist only inside published bar charts and were recovered by two independent extractors from PDF vector coordinates, enumerating all integer candidates within a fixed tolerance and reconciling disagreements against the original sources. Coverage of the interval method was verified on synthetic data before the data-dependent steps, and flagged under-coverage in three conditions led to a registered interpretation rule rather than a change of method. No real-data VE has been computed at the time of registration; the information already viewed is enumerated rather than claimed absent. The protocol states in advance that a sensitivity range crossing zero is part of the intended finding about the limits of conditional inference, and is not evidence that the vaccine is ineffective.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aims to estimate measles vaccine effectiveness (VE) in Japan using public surveillance data, addressing the challenge of accurately assessing VE in an elimination setting where case numbers are small and reference populations may not align with case-generating populations.
Design and population
The study is a protocol for estimating VE using Japanese public surveillance data, focusing on measles cases and vaccination history across three time periods (2008-2014, 2015-2019, 2020-2025), primarily in children aged 2-4 years.
Core methods
The study employs a screening method to estimate VE from published Japanese aggregate data, comparing one or more doses of measles vaccine against none, using a two-group binomial likelihood model with calendar year adjustments and 95 percent profile-likelihood intervals.
Main results
The main results include VE estimates for children aged 2-4 years, with secondary results reported for ages 5-19 years.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as highlighting the limitations of conditional inference in VE estimation, emphasizing that a sensitivity range crossing zero does not imply vaccine ineffectiveness but rather reflects methodological constraints.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance strategies and the interpretation of VE in elimination settings, contributing to improved understanding of vaccine performance and potential biases in data collection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges limitations such as missing vaccination data and potential selection mismatch, but the evidence strength is considered moderate due to the use of synthetic data verification and a registered interpretation rule.

病例报告:麻疹(麻疹)在儿童中的病例

Laporan Kasus: Campak (Measles) pada Anak
作者： Astri Pinilih, Muhammad Ubayya Kholis, Dewica Tiara Ningtyas, Alfiyyah Iftikhar, Angga Saputra, Areza Fahma Addini, Gilang Septian, Nita Shafia Istiana, Mia Santika
摘要中文翻译麻疹(麻疹)是一种高度传染性的急性病毒性传染病,仍然是儿童健康的主要公共卫生问题。该疾病可能导致各种并发症,由于暂时性免疫系统功能下降。本病例报告旨在描述麻疹(麻疹)患者的临床表现、诊断(诊断)及治疗措施。本报告为一项描述性病例报告研究,涉及一名3岁9个月的男童,主诉持续7天的发热,伴有咳嗽、流涕、结膜炎以及从耳后蔓延至全身的斑丘疹。体格检查发现口腔黏膜上有Koplik斑。实验室检查显示白细胞减少伴相对淋巴细胞增多。患者的临床表现符合麻疹(麻疹)的典型特征。患者接受了支持性治疗,包括静脉输液、退热药、止吐药以及高剂量维生素A,治疗期间临床状况有所改善。麻疹(麻疹)的诊断(诊断)可通过典型的临床症状和特征性体征进行,而适当的综合支持治疗对改善患者状况具有良好的效果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
麻疹(麻疹)仍然是儿童公共卫生问题,因此需要进行这项病例报告以记录单一儿科病例的临床表现、诊断(诊断)及治疗措施。
设计与对象
本研究是一项描述性病例报告研究,针对一名3岁9个月的男童,其主诉为持续7天的发热,伴有咳嗽、流涕、结膜炎以及从耳后蔓延至全身的斑丘疹。
核心方法
本报告描述了临床观察、体格检查(包括口腔黏膜上的Koplik斑)、实验室检查结果(白细胞减少伴相对淋巴细胞增多),以及支持性治疗措施(包括静脉输液、退热药、止吐药和高剂量维生素A)。
主要结果
体格检查发现口腔黏膜上有Koplik斑;实验室检查显示白细胞减少伴相对淋巴细胞增多;患者接受了支持性治疗,并在治疗期间表现出临床改善。
解释与创新
作者将Koplik斑解释为支持麻疹(麻疹)临床诊断(诊断)的特征性体征,并得出结论:适当的综合支持治疗可带来良好的临床结果。
科研与公卫意义
基于特征性体征和Koplik斑的临床诊断(诊断)足以确认麻疹(麻疹),而包括高剂量维生素A在内的支持性治疗有助于实现良好的康复效果。
局限与证据强度
本报告仅基于单一儿科病例的摘要,因此证据仅限于一名患者,无法建立患病率、因果关系或超出所描述病例的普遍性。

Laporan Kasus: Campak (Measles) pada Anak

Authors: Astri Pinilih, Muhammad Ubayya Kholis, Dewica Tiara Ningtyas, Alfiyyah Iftikhar, Angga Saputra, Areza Fahma Addini, Gilang Septian, Nita Shafia Istiana, Mia Santika
Original abstract (English)
Campak (measles) merupakan penyakit infeksi virus akut yang sangat menular dan masih menjadi masalah kesehatan masyarakat, terutama pada anak-anak. Penyakit ini dapat menyebabkan berbagai komplikasi akibat penurunan sistem imun sementara. Tujuan laporan kasus ini adalah untuk menggambarkan manifestasi klinis, penegakan diagnosis, serta tatalaksana pada pasien campak. Laporan ini merupakan studi deskriptif dengan desain laporan kasus pada seorang anak laki-laki usia 3 tahun 9 bulan yang datang dengan keluhan demam selama 7 hari disertai batuk, pilek, konjungtivitis, serta ruam makulopapular yang menyebar dari belakang telinga ke seluruh tubuh. Pada pemeriksaan fisik ditemukan bercak Koplik pada mukosa bukal. Pemeriksaan laboratorium menunjukkan leukopenia dengan limfositosis relatif. Manifestasi klinis pasien sesuai dengan gambaran khas campak. Pasien mendapatkan tatalaksana suportif berupa cairan intravena, antipiretik, antiemetik, serta vitamin A dosis tinggi, dan menunjukkan perbaikan klinis selama perawatan. Diagnosis campak dapat ditegakkan secara klinis berdasarkan gejala khas dan tanda patognomonik, serta tatalaksana suportif yang adekuat memberikan hasil yang baik dalam perbaikan kondisi pasien.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Campak (measles) remains a major public-health problem in children, and this case report was needed to document clinical presentation, diagnosis, and management in a single pediatric patient.
Design and population
This is a descriptive case-report study of one 3-year-old boy presenting with fever for seven days, cough, rhinorrhea, conjunctivitis, and a maculopapular rash spreading from the retroauricular region to the whole body.
Core methods
The report describes clinical observation, physical examination including Koplik spots on buccal mucosa, laboratory findings of leukopenia with relative lymphocytosis, and supportive management with intravenous fluids, antipyretics, antiemetics, and high-dose vitamin A.
Main results
Physical examination revealed Koplik spots on buccal mucosa; laboratory tests showed leukopenia with relative lymphocytosis; the patient received supportive care and showed clinical improvement during treatment.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret Koplik spots as a pathognomonic sign supporting clinical diagnosis of measles, and they conclude that adequate supportive therapy yields favorable clinical outcomes.
Scientific and public-health significance
Clinical diagnosis based on characteristic signs and Koplik spots can suffice for measles confirmation, and supportive care including high-dose vitamin A contributes to favorable recovery.
Limitations and evidence strength
This is an abstract-only report from a single pediatric case, so the evidence is limited to one patient and cannot establish prevalence, causality, or generalizability beyond the described case.

HSP90抑制剂对腮腺炎病毒及相关副粘病毒的活性

Antiviral activity of HSP90 inhibitors against Mumps virus and related paramyxoviruses
作者： Fumihiro Kato, Chaewon Bae, Aika Wakata, Masatoshi Kakizaki, Takashi Okura, Yuichiro Nakatsu, Fumio Seki, Kenji Someya, Noriyuki Otsuki, Akihide Ryo
摘要中文翻译摘要 腮腺炎病毒(MuV)是副粘病毒科的一种病毒,尽管其在全球范围内广泛传播,但仍持续引起反复感染。然而,目前尚无获批用于MuV或其他副粘病毒的抗病毒药物。在本研究中,我们使用一种表达绿色荧光蛋白的重组MuV筛选了大约1,000种小分子化合物,并鉴定了六种显著抑制病毒复制且无明显细胞毒性的化合物。这些化合物已知是热休克蛋白90(HSP90)的抑制剂,HSP90是一种参与病毒聚合酶复合物组装和功能的伴侣蛋白。这些HSP90抑制剂表现出强效的抗病毒活性,具有亚微摩尔EC50值和高选择性指数。此外,它们的抗病毒效果还扩展到其他副粘病毒,包括麻疹病毒(MeV)、犬瘟热病毒(CDV)和仙台病毒(SeV)。时间添加实验表明,这些化合物在病毒进入后起作用,主要是在病毒RNA合成阶段。一致地,对MuV和尼帕病毒(NiV)的最小基因组实验确认了对病毒RNA合成的强力抑制,而不会直接抑制报告酶活性,表明其作用具有特异性。综上所述,我们的研究结果突显了HSP90抑制剂作为针对副粘病毒的抗病毒药物的潜力,并支持其进一步开发用于治疗新兴和再新兴的副粘病毒感染。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在识别针对腮腺炎病毒及相关副粘病毒的潜在抗病毒药物,鉴于腮腺炎病毒在全球范围内的持续传播以及目前尚无获批用于这些病原体的抗病毒药物。
设计与对象
本研究通过使用表达绿色荧光蛋白的重组腮腺炎病毒筛选了大约1,000种小分子化合物,以识别那些能够抑制病毒复制且无细胞毒性的化合物。
核心方法
使用表达绿色荧光蛋白的重组腮腺炎病毒筛选化合物,并进行时间添加实验以确定抑制剂对病毒复制阶段的影响。
主要结果
鉴定了六种能够显著抑制病毒复制且无细胞毒性的化合物,其抗病毒效果还扩展到其他副粘病毒,如麻疹病毒、犬瘟热病毒和仙台病毒。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,HSP90抑制剂可能作为针对副粘病毒的抗病毒药物,为治疗新兴和再新兴的副粘病毒感染提供了一种新的方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为进一步开发HSP90抑制剂作为治疗副粘病毒感染的潜在治疗药物奠定了基础,包括腮腺炎病毒。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外研究结果,证据强度中等,因为它不包括临床或体内验证。

Antiviral activity of HSP90 inhibitors against Mumps virus and related paramyxoviruses

Authors: Fumihiro Kato, Chaewon Bae, Aika Wakata, Masatoshi Kakizaki, Takashi Okura, Yuichiro Nakatsu, Fumio Seki, Kenji Someya, Noriyuki Otsuki, Akihide Ryo
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Mumps virus (MuV), a member of the family Paramyxoviridae , continues to cause recurrent outbreaks worldwide despite widespread vaccination. Currently, however, no antiviral drugs have been approved for MuV or other paramyxoviruses. In this study, we screened approximately 1,000 small-molecule compounds using a recombinant MuV expressing green fluorescent protein and identified six compounds that significantly suppressed viral replication without detectable cytotoxicity. These compounds were known inhibitors of heat shock protein 90 (HSP90), a host chaperone involved in the assembly and function of viral polymerase complexes. These HSP90 inhibitors exhibited potent antiviral activity with submicromolar EC50 values and high selectivity indices. Moreover, their antiviral efficacy extended to other paramyxoviruses, including measles virus (MeV), canine distemper virus (CDV), and Sendai virus (SeV). Time-of-addition assays demonstrated that these compounds act at a post-entry stage, primarily during viral RNA synthesis. Consistently, minigenome assays of MuV and Nipah virus (NiV) confirmed robust suppression of viral RNA synthesis without direct inhibition of reporter enzyme activity, indicating target specificity. Collectively, our findings highlight the potential of HSP90 inhibitors as host-directed antiviral agents against paramyxoviruses and support their further development for the treatment of emerging and re-emerging paramyxovirus infections.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify potential antiviral agents against measles virus and related paramyxoviruses, given the ongoing global outbreaks of mumps virus and the lack of approved antiviral drugs for these pathogens.
Design and population
The study involved screening approximately 1,000 small-molecule compounds using a recombinant mumps virus expressing green fluorescent protein to identify those that suppress viral replication without cytotoxicity.
Core methods
A recombinant mumps virus expressing green fluorescent protein was used to screen compounds, and time-of-addition assays were conducted to determine the stage of viral replication affected by the inhibitors.
Main results
Six compounds were identified that significantly suppressed viral replication with no detectable cytotoxicity, and their antiviral efficacy extended to other paramyxoviruses such as measles virus, canine distemper virus, and Sendai virus.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that HSP90 inhibitors could serve as host-directed antiviral agents against paramyxoviruses, offering a new approach for treating emerging and re-emerging infections.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide a foundation for further development of HSP90 inhibitors as potential therapeutic options for paramyxovirus infections, including measles.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro findings, and the evidence strength is moderate as it does not include clinical or in vivo validation.

亚急性硬化性全脑炎 中的神经影像学:揭示毁灭性脑病的谱系

Neuroimaging in subacute sclerosing panencephalitis: unravelling the spectrum of a devastating encephalopathy
作者： Rajarshi Chakraborty, Harish Nigam, Rajesh Verma
摘要中文翻译传统神经影像学 SSPE 中大脑结构磁共振成像 (MRI) 结果的演变谱与疾病分期动态相关,反映了病毒向神经性、神经炎症、继发性脱髓鞘和随后的脑神经胶质增生的解剖进展(表 1)。3 表 1. 亚急性硬化性全脑炎 Sl 中显示的神经影像学结果 编号 阶段 神经影像学发现* 病理机制 1 I 通常正常或微弱的皮质下白色物质 T2/FLAIR 高信号 早期病毒神经侵袭,局部血管周围套箍 2 II 顶枕部白质不对称病变,弥散受限 炎症性脱髓鞘,细胞毒性水肿,小胶质细胞活化 3 III–IV 融合性白质丢失,基底节/脑干病变,弥漫性脑/小脑萎缩 广泛神经元丢失,神经胶质增生,慢性轴突丢失 注:*,常规脑磁共振成像、FLAIR、液体衰减恢复反转;Sl 编号,序列号。疾病早期阶段:在早期阶段,常规 T1 和 T2 加权 MRI 序列可能会产生完全正常的结果,从而延迟怀疑。IP Indian Journal of Neurosciences 2026;12(3):89-90 内容可访问:https://www.ipinnovative.com/open-access-journals IP Indian Journal of Neurosciences 期刊主页:https://ijnonline.org/ 亚急性硬化性全脑炎 中的社论神经影像:揭开毁灭性脑病的谱系 Rajarshi Chakraborty1* iD、Harish Nigam1 iD、Rajesh Verma1 iD 1Dept。神经病学,乔治国王医科大学 (KGMU),勒克瑙,北方邦,印度 收稿: 01-10-2026 接受: 13-10-2026 在线可用: 28-10-2026 这是一本开放获取 (OA) 期刊,文章在 t...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨神经影像学在了解 亚急性硬化性全脑炎 (SSPE)(麻疹病毒 感染的破坏性并发症)的进展和分期中的作用。背景强调,SSPE 是野生型 麻疹病毒 感染的长期后果,其特点是进行性认知衰退、行为异常和最终的自主神经崩溃。
设计与对象
该研究是一项前瞻性随访研究,重点关注诊断为 亚急性硬化性全脑炎 的患者的神经影像异常。它研究了该疾病在不同阶段的进展及其对热带国家儿童和年轻人大脑结构的影响。
核心方法
该研究采用传统和先进的神经影像技术,包括磁共振成像(MRI)、扩散加权成像(DWI)、表观扩散系数(ADC)映射、质子磁共振波谱(MRS)、基于表面的形态测量(SBM)和基于体素的形态测量(VBM)分析来评估SSPE患者的结构和代谢变化。
主要结果
在早期阶段,MRI 结果可能正常或显示微弱的皮质下白质 T2/FLAIR 高信号。随着疾病的进展,可观察到不对称顶枕白质病变和弥散受限。在晚期,会出现融合性白质损失、基底节/脑干病变和弥漫性大脑/小脑萎缩。先进的成像技术显示 N-乙酰天冬氨酸 (NAA)/肌酸 (Cr) 比率降低,胆碱 (Cho)/肌酸 (Cr) 比率升高,表明神经元丢失和活动性脱髓鞘。
解释与创新
作者解释说,神经影像学已经发展成为评估 SSPE 的疾病阶段、进展和非典型变异的关键工具,为仅通过临床症状不易辨别的病理学 进化 提供了见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为神经影像学在 SSPE 分期和鉴别 诊断 中的应用提供了信息,有助于早期发现并指导该疾病流行的热带地区的临床管理。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其重点关注神经影像学结果,没有详细的统计分析或与其他诊断方法的比较。证据是中等的,因为它提供了对成像模式的描述性见解,但缺乏更广泛的验证或对照组。

Neuroimaging in subacute sclerosing panencephalitis: unravelling the spectrum of a devastating encephalopathy

Authors: Rajarshi Chakraborty, Harish Nigam, Rajesh Verma
Original abstract (English)
Conventional neuroimaging The evolving spectrum of structural magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) findings of the brain in SSPE dynamically correlates with disease staging, reflecting the anatomical progression of viral neuro-tropism, neuro-inflammation, secondary demyelination, and subsequent cerebral gliosis (Table 1).3 Table 1. Showing neuroimaging findings in Subacute Sclerosing Panencephalitis Sl No. Stage Neuroimaging finding* Patho-mechanism 1 I Often normal or faint subcortical white matter T2/FLAIR hyperintensities Early viral neuro-invasion, localized perivascular cuffing 2 II Asymmetric parieto-occipital white matter lesions, restricted diffusion Inflammatory demyelination, cytotoxic oedema, microglial activation 3 III–IV Confluent white matter loss, basal ganglia/brainstem lesions, diffuse cerebral/cerebellar atrophy Widespread neuronal loss, gliosis, chronic axonal loss Note: *, conventional magnetic resonance imaging of brain, FLAIR, fluid attenuated recovery inversion; Sl No, serial number. IP Indian Journal of Neurosciences 2026;12(3):89-90 Content available at: https://www.ipinnovative.com/open-access-journals IP Indian Journal of Neurosciences Journal homepage: https://ijnonline.org/ Editorial Neuroimaging in subacute sclerosing panencephalitis: unravelling the spectrum of a devastating encephalopathy Rajarshi Chakraborty1* iD, Harish Nigam1 iD, Rajesh Verma1 iD 1Dept. of Neurology, King George’s Medical University (KGMU), Lucknow, Uttar Pradesh, India Received: 01-10-2026 Accepted: 13-10-2026 Available Online: 28-10-2026 This is an Open Access (OA) journal, and articles are distributed under the t...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to explore the role of neuroimaging in understanding the progression and staging of subacute sclerosing panencephalitis (SSPE), a devastating complication of measles virus infection. The background highlights that SSPE is a long-term consequence of wild-type measles virus infection, marked by progressive cognitive decline, behavioral abnormalities, and eventual autonomic collapse.
Design and population
The study is a prospective follow-up study focusing on neuroimaging abnormalities in patients diagnosed with subacute sclerosing panencephalitis. It examines the disease's progression across different stages and its impact on brain structures in children and young adults in tropical countries.
Core methods
The study employs conventional and advanced neuroimaging techniques, including magnetic resonance imaging (MRI), diffusion-weighted imaging (DWI), apparent diffusion coefficient (ADC) mapping, proton magnetic resonance spectroscopy (MRS), surface-based morphometry (SBM), and voxel-based morphometry (VBM) analysis to assess structural and metabolic changes in SSPE patients.
Main results
In early stages, MRI findings may be normal or show faint subcortical white matter T2/FLAIR hyperintensities. As the disease progresses, asymmetric parieto-occipital white matter lesions and restricted diffusion are observed. In late stages, confluent white matter loss, basal ganglia/brainstem lesions, and diffuse cerebral/cerebellar atrophy are noted. Advanced imaging techniques reveal decreased N-acetyl aspartate (NAA)/Creatine (Cr) ratio and elevated Choline (Cho)/Creatine (Cr) ratio, indicating neuronal loss and active demyelination.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that neuroimaging has evolved into a critical tool for evaluating disease stage, progression, and atypical variants of SSPE, providing insights into the pathological evolution that are not easily discernible through clinical symptoms alone.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the use of neuroimaging in the staging and differential diagnosis of SSPE, aiding in early detection and guiding clinical management in tropical regions where the disease is prevalent.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on neuroimaging findings without detailed statistical analysis or comparison with other diagnostic methods. The evidence is moderate, as it provides descriptive insights into imaging patterns but lacks broader validation or control groups.

杀菌紫外线减少室内呼吸道感染的 传播:系统评价。

Germicidal ultraviolet to reduce transmission of respiratory infections indoors: a systematic review.
作者： Grant P. Higerd-Rusli, Erin C. Stone, Seth A. Hoffman, Ernest R. Blatchley, David T. Kuhar, Stephen P. Luby, Stephen B. Martin, Richard A. Martinello, C Cameron Miller, Katherine Ratliff
摘要中文翻译背景:通过空气传播的呼吸道感染在很大程度上导致了全球发病率和 死亡率。尽管 治疗 空气杀菌紫外线 (GUV) 的临床前数据很有希望,但其在现实世界中的有效性仍不完全清楚。目的:审查 GUV 预防呼吸道感染 传播 的有效性和安全性。数据来源:Medline、Embase、Global Health、Cochrane Library、CINAHL、Engineering Village、Epistemonikos、Scopus 和环境科学馆藏(从开始到 2025 年 12 月)。研究选择:使用比较器进行 GUV 研究,测量人类结果,包括呼吸道感染、缺勤或不良事件。数据提取:至少有两名研究人员提取了数据。数据综合:我们确定了 57 份报告,描述了 1940 年至 2025 年间发表的 40 项研究:30 项非随机对照试验、4 项随机对照试验 (RCT)、4 个病例系列、1 项 队列研究 研究和 1 项横断面研究。非随机研究表明,GUV 可以减少通过空气传播的高传染性感染(麻疹 和水痘),尽管对于未分化的呼吸道疾病和缺勤的结果好坏参半。两项随机对照试验没有得出结论或仅显示出有限的效果。16 项研究的安全监测表明,正确安装的 GUV 不会增加眼睛或皮肤刺激。所有研究都至少存在一项有效性威胁;89% 是非随机的,83% 缺乏统计功效计算。局限性:不同的研究设计、干预措施和结果无法进行荟萃分析。结论:几项较早的临床研究表明,GUV 可减少通过空气传播的高传染性感染,但其针对呼吸道感染的更广泛有效性仍不确定。不确定的结果可能反映了方法学和流行病学的局限性,而不是真正的无效。需要具有强大临床终点的足够有力的随机对照试验。注册:https://osf.io/fg6qx。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
通过空气传播的呼吸道感染在很大程度上导致了全球发病率和 死亡率。本研究旨在评价杀菌紫外线(GUV)预防呼吸道感染 传播 的有效性和安全性。

该研究包括 57 份报告,描述了 1940 年至 2025 年间发表的 40 项研究,其中包括 30 项非随机对照试验、4 项随机对照试验 (RCT)、4 个病例系列、1 项 队列研究 研究和 1 项横断面研究。

非随机研究表明,GUV 可以减少通过空气传播的高传染性感染(麻疹 和水痘),尽管对于未分化的呼吸道疾病和缺勤的结果好坏参半。两项随机对照试验没有得出结论或仅显示出有限的效果。16 项研究的安全监测表明,正确安装的 GUV 不会增加眼睛或皮肤刺激。

几项较早的临床研究表明,GUV 可减少通过空气传播的高传染性感染,但其针对呼吸道感染的更广泛有效性仍不确定。不确定的结果可能反映了方法学和流行病学的局限性,而不是真正的无效。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,GUV 可能是减少 麻疹 等高度传染性呼吸道感染的 传播 的有用工具,但需要进一步的研究来证实其对更广泛的呼吸道病原体的有效性。
局限与证据强度
所有研究都至少存在一项有效性威胁;89% 是非随机的,83% 缺乏统计功效计算。异质的研究设计、干预措施和结果排除了荟萃分析。由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

Germicidal ultraviolet to reduce transmission of respiratory infections indoors: a systematic review.

Authors: Grant P. Higerd-Rusli, Erin C. Stone, Seth A. Hoffman, Ernest R. Blatchley, David T. Kuhar, Stephen P. Luby, Stephen B. Martin, Richard A. Martinello, C Cameron Miller, Katherine Ratliff
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Respiratory infections that transmit through air contribute substantially to global morbidity and mortality. Although preclinical data on germicidal ultraviolet (GUV) for air treatment are promising, its real-world effectiveness remains incompletely understood. PURPOSE: Review effectiveness and safety of GUV for preventing respiratory infection transmission. DATA SOURCES: Medline, Embase, Global Health, Cochrane Library, CINAHL, Engineering Village, Epistemonikos, Scopus, and Environmental Science Collection from inception through December 2025. STUDY SELECTION: Studies of GUV with a comparator, measuring human outcomes including respiratory infections, absenteeism, or adverse events. DATA EXTRACTION: At least two investigators extracted data. DATA SYNTHESIS: We identified 57 reports describing 40 studies published between 1940 and 2025: 30 non-randomized controlled trials, 4 randomized controlled trials (RCTs), 4 case series, 1 cohort study, and 1 cross-sectional study. Non-randomized studies indicated GUV reduces infections with high transmissibility through air (measles and chickenpox), although results were mixed for undifferentiated respiratory illnesses and absenteeism. Two RCTs were inconclusive or showed only modest effects. Safety monitoring in 16 studies identified no increased eye or skin irritation from properly installed GUV. All studies had at least one threat to validity; 89% were non-randomized, and 83% lacked statistical power calculations. LIMITATIONS: Heterogeneous study designs, interventions, and outcomes precluded meta-analysis. CONCLUSIONS: Several older clinical studies indicated that GUV reduces infections with high transmissibility through air, but its broader effectiveness against respiratory infection remains uncertain. Inconclusive results may reflect methodological and epidemiological limitations rather than true ineffectiveness. Adequately powered RCTs with robust clinical endpoints are needed. REGISTRATION: https://osf.io/fg6qx.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory infections that transmit through air contribute substantially to global morbidity and mortality. This study aimed to review the effectiveness and safety of germicidal ultraviolet (GUV) for preventing respiratory infection transmission.
Design and population
The study included 57 reports describing 40 studies published between 1940 and 2025, comprising 30 non-randomized controlled trials, 4 randomized controlled trials (RCTs), 4 case series, 1 cohort study, and 1 cross-sectional study.
Core methods
DATA SOURCES: Medline, Embase, Global Health, Cochrane Library, CINAHL, Engineering Village, Epistemonikos, Scopus, and Environmental Science Collection were used. STUDY SELECTION: Studies of GUV with a comparator, measuring human outcomes including respiratory infections, absenteeism, or adverse events. DATA EXTRACTION: At least two investigators extracted data.
Main results
Non-randomized studies indicated GUV reduces infections with high transmissibility through air (measles and chickenpox), although results were mixed for undifferentiated respiratory illnesses and absenteeism. Two RCTs were inconclusive or showed only modest effects. Safety monitoring in 16 studies identified no increased eye or skin irritation from properly installed GUV.
Interpretation and novelty
Several older clinical studies indicated that GUV reduces infections with high transmissibility through air, but its broader effectiveness against respiratory infection remains uncertain. Inconclusive results may reflect methodological and epidemiological limitations rather than true ineffectiveness.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that GUV may be a useful tool for reducing the transmission of highly transmissible respiratory infections like measles, but further research is needed to confirm its effectiveness against a broader range of respiratory pathogens.
Limitations and evidence strength
All studies had at least one threat to validity; 89% were non-randomized, and 83% lacked statistical power calculations. Heterogeneous study designs, interventions, and outcomes precluded meta-analysis. The evidence strength is moderate due to these limitations.

用于猴痘疾病检测的 CNN-ViT 和 SE-CNN 与 grad-CAM 的混合架构

Hybrid architectures of CNN-ViT and SE-CNN with grad-CAM for monkeypox disease detection
作者： Saeed Mohsen, M. Mokhtar Zayed, Ahmed F. Ibrahim, M. Abdel-Aziz
摘要中文翻译摘要 2019 年冠状病毒病 (COVID-19) 大流行后,猴痘病毒 (MPV) 成为全球重大健康问题,导致世界卫生组织 (WHO) 宣布其为突发公共卫生事件。由于猴痘 (Mpox) 与 麻疹 和水痘等其他痘病毒相似,准确检测猴痘 (Mpox) 具有挑战性。传统的诊断方法通常难以区分这些疾病,因此需要开发更先进的诊断工具。本文提出了一种使用两种高效深度学习(DL)模型来检测 Mpox 的新方法:第一个模型是卷积神经网络视觉变换器(CNN-ViT)的混合架构,第二个模型是挤压和激励卷积神经网络(SE-CNN)。这些模型用于将医学图像分为两类:猴痘和其他。所提出的模型应用于 1140 张图像的数据集,并对模型的超参数进行微调。数据增强技术用于训练图像,以最大限度地提高模型的性能。此外,应用梯度加权类激活映射(Grad-CAM)可视化技术来解释模型的决策并帮助医生进行临床决策。实验上,使用准确度、误差矩阵、受试者工作特征(ROC)曲线下面积、召回率、F1 分数和精度来衡量所提出模型的性能。CNN-ViT和SE-CNN的测试精度分别为97.80%和96.05%,而ROC曲线下的微平均面积CNN-ViT为97%,SE-CNN为99%。CNN-ViT 的加权 F1 分数、精度和召回率分别为 97.26%、97.33% 和 97.27%,而 SE-CNN 分别为 96.04%、96.13% 和 96.05%。所提出的模型提供了一种快速、准确的方法来区分 Mpox 与其他病例,为医疗保健专业人员进行 Mpox 筛查和 诊断 提供了宝贵的工具。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究的动机是需要改进猴痘疾病的检测方法,猴痘疾病在 COVID-19 大流行后已成为一个重大的全球健康问题。由于其与 麻疹 和水痘等其他痘病毒相似,世界卫生组织宣布其为公共卫生紧急事件。
设计与对象
该研究采用混合深度学习模型方法使用医学图像检测猴痘。这些模型在包​​含 1140 张图像的数据集上进行了测试,目的是将它们分为两类:猴痘和其他。
核心方法
这些模型利用超参数微调和数据增强技术来提高性能。此外,应用梯度加权类激活映射(Grad-CAM)来可视化模型决策并支持临床决策。
主要结果
CNN-ViT模型的测试准确率达到97.80%,而SE-CNN模型的测试准确率达到96.05%。CNN-ViT 的 ROC 曲线下微平均面积为 97%,SE-CNN 为 99%。CNN-ViT 的加权 F1 分数、精确度和召回率分别为 97.26%、97.33% 和 97.27%,SE-CNN 分别为 96.04%、96.13% 和 96.05%。
解释与创新
作者将这些结果解释为证明了所提出的模型在区分猴痘与其他病例方面的有效性,强调了它们作为医疗保健专业人员筛查和 诊断 的宝贵工具的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,这些模型有助于更快速、更准确地检测猴痘,这对于有效的 监测 和疾病的临床管理至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,结果基于 1140 张图像的数据集。由于缺乏详细的临床验证和数据集中的潜在偏差,证据强度中等。

Hybrid architectures of CNN-ViT and SE-CNN with grad-CAM for monkeypox disease detection

Authors: Saeed Mohsen, M. Mokhtar Zayed, Ahmed F. Ibrahim, M. Abdel-Aziz
Original abstract (English)
Abstract In the aftermath of the coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) pandemic, the monkeypox virus (MPV) emerged as a significant global health concern, leading the World Health Organization (WHO) to declare it a public health emergency. Accurate detection of monkeypox (Mpox) is challenging due to its similarity to other pox viruses such as measles and chickenpox. Conventional diagnostic methods often struggle to distinguish between these diseases, necessitating the development of more advanced diagnostic tools. This paper presents a novel approach to detect Mpox using two efficient deep learning (DL) models: the first one is a hybrid architecture of a Convolutional Neural Network—Vision Transformer (CNN–ViT), and the second model is a Squeeze-and-Excitation Convolutional Neural Network (SE-CNN). These models are employed for the classification of medical images into two categories: Monkeypox and Others. The proposed models are applied to a dataset of 1140 images, and utilize fine-tuning for the models’ hyperparameters. Data augmentation techniques are implemented for training images to maximize the models’ performance. Additionally, a gradient-weighted class activation mapping (Grad-CAM) visualization technique is applied to explain the decision of the models and aid doctors in clinical decision-making. Experimentally, the accuracy, error matrix, area under the receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curve, Recall, F1-score, and Precision are used to measure the performance of the proposed models. The CNN–ViT and SE-CNN achieve a testing accuracy of 97.80% and 96.05%, respectively, while the micro-average area under the ROC curve is 97% for CNN–ViT and 99% for SE-CNN. The weighted F1-score, Precision, and Recall for CNN–ViT are all 97.26%, 97.33%, and 97.27%, respectively, while for SE-CNN are 96.04%, 96.13%, and 96.05%, respectively. The proposed models provide a rapid and accurate method to differentiate Mpox from other cases, offering a valuable tool for healthcare professionals in the screening and diagnosis of Mpox.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was motivated by the need for improved detection methods for monkeypox disease, which has emerged as a significant global health concern following the COVID-19 pandemic. The World Health Organization declared it a public health emergency due to its similarity to other pox viruses like measles and chickenpox.
Design and population
The study employed a hybrid deep learning model approach to detect monkeypox using medical images. The models were tested on a dataset of 1140 images, with the goal of classifying them into two categories: Monkeypox and Others.
Core methods
The models utilized fine-tuning for hyperparameters and data augmentation techniques to enhance performance. Additionally, gradient-weighted class activation mapping (Grad-CAM) was applied to visualize model decisions and support clinical decision-making.
Main results
The CNN–ViT model achieved a testing accuracy of 97.80%, while the SE-CNN model achieved 96.05%. The micro-average area under the ROC curve was 97% for CNN–ViT and 99% for SE-CNN. The weighted F1-score, Precision, and Recall for CNN–ViT were all 97.26%, 97.33%, and 97.27%, respectively, and for SE-CNN were 96.04%, 96.13%, and 96.05%, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these results as demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed models in differentiating monkeypox from other cases, highlighting their potential as a valuable tool for healthcare professionals in screening and diagnosis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that these models can contribute to more rapid and accurate detection of monkeypox, which is critical for effective surveillance and clinical management of the disease.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the results are based on a dataset of 1140 images. The evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of detailed clinical validation and potential biases in the dataset.

从稀疏数据到智能决策:通过模拟评估特定区域的政策

From sparse data to smart decisions: region-specific policy evaluation via simulation
作者： Carson Dudley, Daniel R. Bergman, Harsh Jain, Kerri‐Ann Norton, Erica M. Rutter, Marisa C. Eisenberg, Trachette Levon Jackson
摘要中文翻译有效的传染病应对措施需要针对特定​​区域的指导,因为推动当地 传播 和塑造干预效果的接触结构因社区而异。基于代理的模型(ABM)代表了这些结构以及它们背后的个体异质性,这使得它们非常适合局部干预分析,但它们的计算成本很高并且难以直接拟合数据。我们提出了一个框架,通过使用代理常微分方程模型作为常规 监测 数据和机械丰富的 ABM 之间的中介,避免直接 ABM 拟合。使用加权最小二乘法将替代项拟合到观察到的病例和住院数据,并使用轮廓似然来定义数据一致的 ABM 参数区域。评估该区域的干预效果,同时通过重复模拟单独量化随机 ABM 变异性。我们将该框架应用于密歇根州九个人口结构不同的县的 COVID-19,作为说明性案例研究。对于每个县,我们恢复与当地病例和住院数据一致的 ABM 参数区域,验证校准模型是否再现了观察到的轨迹,并评估 ABM 参数区域的四种非药物干预措施。各县干预措施的排名和稳健性差异很大,简单的人口预测因素无法可靠地预测哪些干预措施会成功。尽管我们使用从 COVID-19 文献中得出的干预实施假设,但该框架是通用的,可以采用用户指定的假设来评估它们暗示的结论。通过将机械 ABM 与明确的不确定性传播相结合,该方法为针对 COVID-19、流感、麻疹 和其他病原体进行局部、不确定性感知干预分析提供了通用途径。

问题与背景
有效的传染病应对措施需要针对特定​​区域的指导,因为推动当地 传播 和塑造干预效果的接触结构因社区而异。
设计与对象
基于代理的模型(ABM)代表了这些结构以及它们背后的个体异质性,这使得它们非常适合局部干预分析,但它们的计算成本很高并且难以直接拟合数据。
核心方法
我们提出了一个框架,通过使用代理常微分方程模型作为常规 监测 数据和机械丰富的 ABM 之间的中介,避免直接 ABM 拟合。
主要结果
使用加权最小二乘法将替代项拟合到观察到的病例和住院数据,并使用轮廓似然来定义数据一致的 ABM 参数区域。
解释与创新
评估该区域的干预效果,同时通过重复模拟单独量化随机 ABM 变异性。
科研与公卫意义
我们将该框架应用于密歇根州九个人口结构不同的县的 COVID-19,作为说明性案例研究。
局限与证据强度
各县干预措施的排名和稳健性差异很大,简单的人口预测因素无法可靠地预测哪些干预措施会成功。

From sparse data to smart decisions: region-specific policy evaluation via simulation

Authors: Carson Dudley, Daniel R. Bergman, Harsh Jain, Kerri‐Ann Norton, Erica M. Rutter, Marisa C. Eisenberg, Trachette Levon Jackson
Original abstract (English)
Effective infectious disease response requires region-specific guidance, because the contact structures that drive local transmission and shape intervention effects vary from community to community. Agent-based models (ABMs) represent these structures and the individual heterogeneity underlying them, which makes them well suited to local intervention analysis, but they are computationally expensive and difficult to fit directly to data. We present a framework that avoids direct ABM fitting by using a surrogate ordinary differential equation model as an intermediary between routine surveillance data and a mechanistically rich ABM. The surrogate is fit to observed case and hospitalization data using weighted least squares, with profile likelihood used to define a data-consistent ABM parameter region. Intervention effects are evaluated across this region, while stochastic ABM variability is quantified separately through repeated simulations. We apply the framework to COVID-19 in nine demographically distinct Michigan counties as an illustrative case study. For each county, we recover ABM parameter regions consistent with local case and hospitalization data, verify that the calibrated model reproduces observed trajectories, and evaluate four non-pharmaceutical interventions across the ABM parameter regions. The ranking and robustness of interventions differ substantially across counties, and simple demographic predictors do not reliably predict which will succeed. Although we use intervention implementation assumptions drawn from COVID-19 literature, the framework is general and can take user-specified assumptions to evaluate the conclusions they imply. By coupling mechanistic ABMs with explicit uncertainty propagation, this approach provides a generic route to local, uncertainty-aware intervention analysis for COVID-19, influenza, measles, and other pathogens.
View research seven-element analysis

Effective infectious disease response requires region-specific guidance, because the contact structures that drive local transmission and shape intervention effects vary from community to community.

美国佛罗里达州主要大都市区医生关于病毒疹 诊断 的知识、态度和实践以及强制报告要求

Physician Knowledge, Attitudes, and Practices Regarding Viral Exanthem Diagnosis and Mandatory Reporting Requirements in Major Metropolitan Regions of Florida, USA
作者： Chen W, Ballarin S, Fioletova M, Bhosale C, Matthews T, Blavo C, Forbes J, Potter A
摘要中文翻译此外,在现代临床实践中,疫苗 可预防疾病相对罕见,可能会降低医生对这些感染的熟悉度。方法这项 IRB 豁免横断面研究使用包含 19 个问题的研究电子数据采集(REDCap;美国纳什维尔范德比尔特大学)调查评估了佛罗里达州医生关于 VE 疫苗 和报告要求的知识、态度和实践 (KAP)。这些发现确定了额外的医生教育可能有益的领域,特别是在病毒疹的临床区分和强制性报告要求方面。材料和方法总体研究设计这项 IRB 豁免横断面研究采用了一项包含 19 个问题的在线调查,其中包括 6 个人口统计/专业问题、6 个知识问题、5 个态度问题和 2 个练习问题。本研究的方法与初步研究[19]密切相关,并进行了修改以解决当前调查的具体目标。本研究的纳入标准是持有有效执照的医生(DO 或 MD)或拥有医疗培训执照的住院医师。背景病毒性皮疹 (VE),包括 疫苗 可预防的感染,如 麻疹 和风疹,以及非 疫苗 可预防的感染,如玫瑰疹,仍然具有临床相关性。疫苗接种 费率的下降和最近的 暴发 增加了及时 诊断 和强制报告的重要性。参与者包括家庭医学、儿科、内科和传染病专业的医生和住院医师。统计分析包括方差分析 (ANOVA)、多变量方差分析 (MANOVA)、Tukey 事后检验和线性回归。结果共有 162 名医生完成...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估佛罗里达州医生对病毒性疹 (VE) 诊断 和强制报告要求的知识、态度和实践,强调需要了解临床对这些疾病认识的差距。
设计与对象
对佛罗里达州 162 名医生进行了一项横断面调查,其中 146 名医生提供了用于推理分析的完整回答。参与者是根据许可证状态、县和专业从佛罗里达州卫生部从业者数据库中确定的。
核心方法
统计分析包括方差分析 (ANOVA)、多元方差分析 (MANOVA)、Tukey 事后检验和线性回归。通过线性回归分析小插图式 诊断 知识问题和信心态度问题之间的关系。
主要结果
儿科医生的总体知识得分最高(82.66%),其次是内科医生(76.39%)和家庭医生(73.26%)。在 162 份回复中,地理实践环境包括城市 83 份(51.2%)、郊区 83 份(51.2%)和农村 18 份(11.1%)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,各专业之间的总体知识差异显着,儿科医生的得分高于家庭医生。然而,当单独检查基于小插图的知识问题时,没有发现显着差异。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,需要对医生(特别是家庭医学领域的医生)开展有针对性的培训计划,以提高他们在诊断和报告 麻疹 等病毒疹方面的知识和信心。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到非随机受访者样本、不均匀的专业代表性和潜在的不答复偏差的限制。由于这些限制,证据是中等的。

Physician Knowledge, Attitudes, and Practices Regarding Viral Exanthem Diagnosis and Mandatory Reporting Requirements in Major Metropolitan Regions of Florida, USA

Authors: Chen W, Ballarin S, Fioletova M, Bhosale C, Matthews T, Blavo C, Forbes J, Potter A
Original abstract (English)
In addition, the relative rarity of vaccine-preventable diseases in modern clinical practice may reduce physician familiarity with these infections.MethodsThis IRB-exempt cross-sectional study assessed the knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) of Florida physicians regarding VE diagnosis and reporting requirements using a 19-question Research Electronic Data Capture (REDCap; Vanderbilt University, Nashville, USA) survey. These findings identify areas in which additional physician education may be beneficial, particularly regarding the clinical differentiation of viral exanthems and mandatory reporting requirements. Materials and methodsOverall study designThis IRB-exempt cross-sectional study utilized an online 19-question survey that consisted of six demographic/professional questions, six knowledge questions, five attitude questions, and two practice questions. The methodology for this study was closely modeled after that of a preliminary study [19], with modifications to address the specific aims of the present investigation. The inclusion criteria for this study were actively licensed physicians (DO or MD) or residents with a medical training license. BackgroundViral exanthems (VEs), including vaccine-preventable infections such as measles and rubella and non-vaccine-preventable infections such as roseola, remain clinically relevant. Declining vaccination rates and recent outbreaks have increased the importance of timely diagnosis and mandatory reporting. Participants included physicians and residents in family medicine, pediatrics, internal medicine, and infectious disease specialties. Statistical analyses included analysis of variance (ANOVA), multivariate ANOVA (MANOVA), Tukey post hoc testing, and linear regression.ResultsA total of 162 physicians completed...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess physician knowledge, attitudes, and practices regarding viral exanthem (VE) diagnosis and mandatory reporting requirements in Florida, highlighting the need to understand gaps in clinical awareness of these conditions.
Design and population
A cross-sectional survey was conducted among 162 physicians in Florida, with 146 providing complete responses for inferential analyses. Participants were identified from the Florida Department of Health practitioner database based on license status, county, and specialty.
Core methods
Statistical analyses included analysis of variance (ANOVA), multivariate ANOVA (MANOVA), Tukey post hoc testing, and linear regression. The relationships between vignette-style diagnosis knowledge questions and confidence attitude questions were analyzed through linear regression.
Main results
Pediatricians demonstrated the highest overall knowledge scores (82.66%), followed by internal medicine (76.39%) and family medicine physicians (73.26%). Of 162 responses, geographic practice settings included urban 83 (51.2%), suburban 83 (51.2%), and rural 18 (11.1%).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that overall knowledge differences among specialties were significant, with pediatricians scoring higher than family medicine physicians. However, no significant differences were found when examining vignette-based knowledge questions separately.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for targeted training programs for physicians, particularly in family medicine, to improve their knowledge and confidence in diagnosing and reporting viral exanthems such as measles.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the non-random respondent sample, uneven specialty representation, and potential nonresponse bias. The evidence is moderate due to these limitations.

麻疹 传播 相关流行病学参数估计:系统评价和荟萃分析。

Measles transmission-related epidemiological parameter estimates: a systematic review and meta-analysis.
作者： Kusala Pussegoda, Mavra Qamar, Melanie Sterian, Austyn Baumeister, Tricia Corrin, Abhinand Thaivalappil, Lisa A. Waddell
摘要中文翻译摘要:随着最近全球 麻疹 暴发 的复苏,了解 麻疹 传播 动态对于指导及时有效的公共卫生应对措施和参数化传染病模型以进行准确的 麻疹 预测至关重要。本系统综述旨在提供 麻疹 传播 相关流行病学参数的综合资源。检索从开始到2024年5月14日进行。纳入了传播相关流行病学参数的英语和法语初步研究,并遵循标准系统评价方法。使用随机效应荟萃分析和描述性图合成证据。按世界卫生组织 (WHO) 地区、人口、十年和报告质量进行亚组分析,以探讨异质性。1910 年至 2024 年间,报告了 612 项研究中 32 个 传播 相关流行病学参数的 1 209 项估计值。麻疹 的特点是合并潜伏期(13.5 天,95% CI 12.6–14.4;I2 = 95.9%)、连续间隔(12.4 天,95% CI 11.5–13.4;I2 = 97.6%)、症状出现到住院(4.8 天,95% CI 3.7–5.8,I2 = 99.7%),基本再生数中位数为 11.5(范围为 0-57)。这项工作是全球流行病防范和应对项目的一部分,该项目旨在建立一个可访问的全球流行病学参数存储库,旨在减少识别和总结流行病学参数的重复工作。

范围与问题
本系统综述旨在提供 麻疹 传播 相关流行病学参数的综合资源。
证据基础与方法
包括英语和法语对 传播 相关流行病学参数的初步研究,并遵循标准系统评价方法。
共识与结论
麻疹 的特点是合并潜伏期(13.5 天,95% CI 12.6–14.4;I2 = 95.9%)、连续间隔(12.4 天,95% CI 11.5–13.4;I2 = 97.6%)、症状出现到住院(4.8 天,95% CI 3.7–5.8,I2 = 99.7%),基本再生数中位数为 11.5(范围为 0-57)。

按世界卫生组织 (WHO) 地区、人口、十年和报告质量进行亚组分析,以探讨异质性。

这项工作是全球流行病防范和应对项目的一部分,该项目旨在建立一个可访问的全球流行病学参数存储库,旨在减少识别和总结流行病学参数的重复工作。

Measles transmission-related epidemiological parameter estimates: a systematic review and meta-analysis.

Authors: Kusala Pussegoda, Mavra Qamar, Melanie Sterian, Austyn Baumeister, Tricia Corrin, Abhinand Thaivalappil, Lisa A. Waddell
Original abstract (English)
Abstract With the recent resurgence of global measles outbreaks, it is crucial to understand measles transmission dynamics to guide timely and effective public health responses and parameterize infectious disease models for accurate outbreak projections. This systematic review aims to provide a comprehensive resource of measles transmission-related epidemiological parameters. A search was conducted from inception to 14 May 2024. English and French primary studies of transmission-related epidemiological parameters were included, and standard systematic review methodology was followed. Evidence was synthesized using random-effects meta-analyses and descriptive plots. Subgroup analyses by World Health Organization (WHO) region, population, decade, and quality of reporting were conducted to explore heterogeneity. Between 1910 and 2024, 1 209 estimates across 32 transmission-related epidemiological parameters from 612 studies were reported. Measles was characterized by a pooled incubation period (13.5 days, 95%CI 12.6–14.4; I2 = 95.9%), serial interval (12.4 days, 95%CI 11.5–13.4; I2 = 97.6%), symptom onset to hospitalization (4.8 days, 95%CI 3.7–5.8, I2 = 99.7%), and basic reproduction number median 11.5 (range of 0–57). This work is part of a global epidemic preparedness and response project to establish an accessible living Global Repository of Epidemiological Parameters aimed at reducing duplicative efforts in identifying and summarizing epidemiological parameters.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This systematic review aims to provide a comprehensive resource of measles transmission-related epidemiological parameters.
Evidence base and method
English and French primary studies of transmission-related epidemiological parameters were included, and standard systematic review methodology was followed.
Consensus and conclusions
Measles was characterized by a pooled incubation period (13.5 days, 95%CI 12.6–14.4; I2 = 95.9%), serial interval (12.4 days, 95%CI 11.5–13.4; I2 = 97.6%), symptom onset to hospitalization (4.8 days, 95%CI 3.7–5.8, I2 = 99.7%), and basic reproduction number median 11.5 (range of 0–57).
Controversies and gaps
Subgroup analyses by World Health Organization (WHO) region, population, decade, and quality of reporting were conducted to explore heterogeneity.
Research and practice implications
This work is part of a global epidemic preparedness and response project to establish an accessible living Global Repository of Epidemiological Parameters aimed at reducing duplicative efforts in identifying and summarizing epidemiological parameters.

麻疹 疫苗 失败和突破性感染的决定因素:基于 IgG 亲合力的免疫标记物和 宿主 遗传多态性的系统评价。

Determinants of Measles Vaccine Failure and Breakthrough Infections: A Systematic Review of IgG Avidity Based Immunological Markers and Host Genetic Polymorphisms.
作者： Gul Zaman, Sahar Shabbir, Daisha Rathod, Amna Sabir, Fatima Sohail, Muhammad Abdullah, Aaira Taharat, Hibah Faisal Ghauri, Santosh Basyal, Rahul Parajuli
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 尽管报道的覆盖率很高,但 麻疹 仍然是可预防的 麻疹 的主要原因。麻疹 疫苗 故障的决定因素仍未完全表征。方法 从一开始就检索了五个数据库(PubMed/MEDLINE、Embase、Web of Science、Scopus、Cochrane Library)。包括评估 IgG 亲和力标记或 宿主 遗传多态性的已接种疫苗个体的研究。使用纽卡斯尔-渥太华量表、JBI 检查表和修改后的 HuGENet 框架评估偏倚风险。研究结果以叙述方式综合(PROSPERO:CRD420261415732)。结果 16 项研究(8169 名受试者;1998-2025 年)符合纳入标准。在七项事件级数据研究中,主要 疫苗 失败率范围为 24.2% 至 90.8%。在 HLA I/II 类等位基因、麻疹 受体基因(SLAM/CD150、CD46、CD209)、TLR 通路基因、抗病毒药物 效应基因和细胞因子/细胞因子受体基因中观察到可重复的 宿主 遗传关联。没有研究报告可提取的突破性感染或二分 IgG 亲合力数据。结论 麻疹 抗体反应簇在 HLA 基因座、麻疹 受体轴和 TLR 通路上的可重复 麻疹 遗传决定因素。不一致的定义和缺乏标准化报告无法得出明确的结论。需要进行前瞻性协调研究。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
尽管报道的覆盖率很高,但 麻疹病毒 仍然是 疫苗 可预防的 死亡率 的主要原因。该综述旨在确定 麻疹 疫苗 失败和突破性感染的决定因素,重点关注 IgG 亲合力标记和 宿主 遗传多态性。
证据基础与方法
从一开始就检索了五个数据库(PubMed/MEDLINE、Embase、Web of Science、Scopus、Cochrane Library)。包括评估 IgG 亲和力标记或 宿主 遗传多态性的已接种疫苗个体的研究。使用纽卡斯尔-渥太华量表、JBI 检查表和修改后的 HuGENet 框架评估偏倚风险。研究结果以叙述方式综合(PROSPERO:CRD420261415732)。
共识与结论
十六项研究(8169 名受试者;1998-2025 年)符合纳入标准。在使用事件级数据的七项研究中,主要 疫苗 失败率范围为 24.2% 至 90.8%。在 HLA I/II 类等位基因、麻疹 受体基因(SLAM/CD150、CD46、CD209)、TLR 通路基因、抗病毒药物 效应基因和细胞因子/细胞因子受体基因中观察到可重复的 宿主 遗传关联。没有研究报告可提取的突破性感染或二分 IgG 亲合力数据。HLA 位点、麻疹 受体轴和 TLR 通路上 麻疹 抗体反应簇的可重复 宿主 遗传决定因素。
争议与缺口
不一致的定义和缺乏标准化报告无法得出明确的结论。需要进行前瞻性协调研究。
科研与实践启示
需要在更大、更多样化的人群中复制研究结果。标准化报告和定义对于未来的研究至关重要。需要统一的前瞻性研究来更好地了解 麻疹 疫苗 失败和突破性感染的决定因素。

Determinants of Measles Vaccine Failure and Breakthrough Infections: A Systematic Review of IgG Avidity Based Immunological Markers and Host Genetic Polymorphisms.

Authors: Gul Zaman, Sahar Shabbir, Daisha Rathod, Amna Sabir, Fatima Sohail, Muhammad Abdullah, Aaira Taharat, Hibah Faisal Ghauri, Santosh Basyal, Rahul Parajuli
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background Measles remains a leading cause of vaccine‐preventable mortality, despite high reported coverage. Determinants of measles vaccine failure remain incompletely characterized. Methods Five databases (PubMed/MEDLINE, Embase, Web of Science, Scopus, Cochrane Library) were searched from inception. Studies in vaccinated individuals evaluating IgG avidity markers or host genetic polymorphisms were included. Risk of bias was assessed using the Newcastle–Ottawa Scale, JBI checklist and modified HuGENet framework. Findings were synthesized narratively (PROSPERO: CRD420261415732). Results Sixteen studies (8169 participants; 1998–2025) met inclusion criteria. Across seven studies with event‐level data, primary vaccine failure ranged from 24.2% to 90.8%. Reproducible host genetic associations were observed for HLA Class I/II alleles, measles receptor genes (SLAM/CD150, CD46, CD209), TLR pathway genes, antiviral effector genes and cytokine/cytokine receptor genes. No study reported extractable breakthrough infection or dichotomous IgG avidity data. Conclusion Reproducible host genetic determinants of measles antibody response cluster at HLA loci, the measles receptor axis and the TLR pathway. Inconsistent definitions and absent standardized reporting preclude firm conclusions. Prospective harmonized studies are required.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Measles virus remains a leading cause of vaccine-preventable mortality, despite high reported coverage. The review aims to identify determinants of measles vaccine failure and breakthrough infections, focusing on IgG avidity markers and host genetic polymorphisms.
Evidence base and method
Five databases (PubMed/MEDLINE, Embase, Web of Science, Scopus, Cochrane Library) were searched from inception. Studies in vaccinated individuals evaluating IgG avidity markers or host genetic polymorphisms were included. Risk of bias was assessed using the Newcastle–Ottawa Scale, JBI checklist, and modified HuGENet framework. Findings were synthesized narratively (PROSPERO: CRD420261415732).
Consensus and conclusions
Sixteen studies (8169 participants; 1998–2025) met inclusion criteria. Across seven studies with event-level data, primary vaccine failure ranged from 24.2% to 90.8%. Reproducible host genetic associations were observed for HLA Class I/II alleles, measles receptor genes (SLAM/CD150, CD46, CD209), TLR pathway genes, antiviral effector genes, and cytokine/cytokine receptor genes. No study reported extractable breakthrough infection or dichotomous IgG avidity data. Reproducible host genetic determinants of measles antibody response cluster at HLA loci, the measles receptor axis, and the TLR pathway.
Controversies and gaps
Inconsistent definitions and absent standardized reporting preclude firm conclusions. Prospective harmonized studies are required.
Research and practice implications
Replication of findings in larger, more diverse populations is needed. Standardized reporting and definitions are essential for future research. Harmonized prospective studies are required to better understand the determinants of measles vaccine failure and breakthrough infections.

五岁以下儿童在低收入和中等收入国家中麻疹后并发症及中麻疹相关疾病:一项叙述性综述

Post‐Measles Complications and Mortality in Children under Five Years in Low‐ and Middle‐Income Countries: A Narrative Review
作者： Abhijeet V. Jadhav, Sankalp Ghadei, Sanket Sonawane, Pushkar Patil, Prashant Birajdar, Avinash R. Deoshatwar, Kunal H. Pise, Amol Nath, Shailesh D. Pawar, Naveen Kumar
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦于中麻疹、中麻疹相关疾病及其在低收入和中等收入国家(LMICs)中五岁以下儿童中的并发症。核心问题是探讨中麻疹后并发症(PMCs)的流行病学证据、其发病机制以及在脆弱人群中实施综合的中麻疹后护理策略的必要性。
证据基础与方法
本叙述性综述纳入了过去55年间通过PubMed和Google Scholar识别出的所有发表有关任何PMCs原始数据的论文。综述未报告荟萃分析或汇总估计,而是综合现有文献以回答研究问题。
共识与结论
流行病学数据显示,在低收入和中等收入国家(LMICs)中,30–40%的中麻疹病例会发展为并发症,包括肺炎、腹泻、中耳炎,以及较少见的脑炎和眼部疾病。由于这些并发症通常在急性感染后一段时间才出现,因此常被误认为是独立的疾病。中麻疹引发的免疫记忆丧失使受影响个体在感染后2–3年内更容易受到二次感染。在LMICs和冲突影响的环境中,这种长期的免疫脆弱性与已有的风险因素如营养不良、微量营养素缺乏和医疗资源获取困难相互作用,显著加剧了后果。
争议与缺口
综述指出,由于PMCs的延迟发病,它们常被误认为是独立的疾病。此外,综述还指出,在资源有限和冲突影响的环境中,中麻疹的消除仍面临挑战,这可能与PMCs的持续存在有关。目前的证据并未报告不同中麻疹基因型或地区中PMCs的相对频率或严重程度的具体数据,表明在理解PMCs的地理和遗传变异性方面仍存在知识空白。
科研与实践启示
迫切需要从全球卫生角度实施综合的中麻疹后护理策略,尤其是在资源有限和冲突影响的环境中。综述强调了应对中麻疹引发的免疫记忆丧失导致的长期免疫脆弱性的重要性,并指出需要在脆弱人群中改善对PMCs的诊断、监测和治疗。

Post‐Measles Complications and Mortality in Children under Five Years in Low‐ and Middle‐Income Countries: A Narrative Review

Authors: Abhijeet V. Jadhav, Sankalp Ghadei, Sanket Sonawane, Pushkar Patil, Prashant Birajdar, Avinash R. Deoshatwar, Kunal H. Pise, Amol Nath, Shailesh D. Pawar, Naveen Kumar
Original abstract (English)
Background: Global measles vaccination programs have achieved significant reductions in measles‐related mortality, with World Health Organization (WHO)‐led initiatives expanding vaccine coverage to over 85% of children worldwide. Despite this progress, measles elimination remains elusive, undermined by anti‐vaccine sentiment, armed conflicts, migration, refugee crises, and post‐COVID‐19 disruptions. No WHO region has achieved or sustained measles elimination status since the COVID‐19 pandemic, and elimination remains challenging in resource‐limited and conflict‐affected contexts. A critical yet underappreciated consequence of this ongoing burden is the rise of post‐measles complications (PMCs). Vaccine‐preventable diseases and malnutrition constitute two critical global health priorities; PMCs represent an important intersection of these agendas that warrant greater attention within the global health framework. Objectives: To review and synthesize current epidemiological evidence on PMCs, examine their pathogenesis, and understand the need for integrated post‐measles care strategies in vulnerable populations. Methods: This narrative review included all papers that published primary data on any of the PMCs in the last 55 years, identified through PubMed and Google Scholar. Findings: Epidemiological data show that 30–40% of measles cases in low‐ and middle‐income countries (LMICs) develop complications, including pneumonia, diarrhea, otitis media, and, less commonly, encephalitis and ocular disease. PMCs are frequently misattributed to independent illnesses because they often emerge after a delay following the acute infection. Compounding this, measles‐induced immune amnesia renders affected individuals vulnerable to secondary infections for up to 2–3 years post‐infection. In LMICs and conflict‐affected settings, this prolonged immunological vulnerability intersects with pre‐existing risk factors such as malnutrition, micronutrient deficiencies, and poor healthcare access, which amplify the consequences substantially. Conclusions: As measles cases resurge globally, the associated burden of PMCs is expected to increase correspondingly. Where PMCs represent a pressing, underaddressed concern, integrated post‐measles care strategies are urgently needed from a global health perspective, especially in resource‐limited and conflict‐affected settings.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the measles virus, measles disease, and its complications in children under five years in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs). The central question is to examine the epidemiological evidence on post-measles complications (PMCs), their pathogenesis, and the need for integrated post-measles care strategies in vulnerable populations.
Evidence base and method
This narrative review included all papers that published primary data on any of the PMCs in the last 55 years, identified through PubMed and Google Scholar. The review does not report a meta-analysis or pooled estimate but synthesizes existing literature to address the research question.
Consensus and conclusions
Epidemiological data show that 30–40% of measles cases in LMICs develop complications, including pneumonia, diarrhea, otitis media, and, less commonly, encephalitis and ocular disease. PMCs are frequently misattributed to independent illnesses because they often emerge after a delay following the acute infection. Measles-induced immune amnesia renders affected individuals vulnerable to secondary infections for up to 2–3 years post-infection. In LMICs and conflict-affected settings, this prolonged immunological vulnerability intersects with pre-existing risk factors such as malnutrition, micronutrient deficiencies, and poor healthcare access, which amplify the consequences substantially.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights that PMCs are frequently misattributed to independent illnesses due to their delayed onset. Additionally, the review notes that measles elimination remains challenging in resource-limited and conflict-affected contexts, which may contribute to the persistence of PMCs. The evidence does not report specific data on the relative frequency or severity of PMCs across different measles virus genotypes or regions, indicating a gap in understanding the geographic and genetic variability of PMCs.
Research and practice implications
Integrated post-measles care strategies are urgently needed from a global health perspective, especially in resource-limited and conflict-affected settings. The review emphasizes the importance of addressing the prolonged immunological vulnerability caused by measles-induced immune amnesia and the need for improved surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment of PMCs in vulnerable populations.

多发性硬化症和病毒:《危险关系》。

Multiple Sclerosis and Viruses: « Liaisons Dangereuses ».
作者： Samanta Plavina, Daniela Malakovska, Jolanta Kalnina, Ilva Trapina, Elina Leonova, Mohammed Habib, Susanne Schifmann, Jegors Paramonovs, Nikolajs Sjakste, Yegor Vassetzky
摘要中文翻译多发性硬化症(MS)是一种复杂的、多因素的神经退行性疾病,其病因仍部分不明。与MS发病相关的风险因素包括遗传易感性和多种环境应激因素;然而,最近病毒感染引起了广泛关注,被认为是重要的环境诱因。尽管已知EB病毒(EBV)与MS易感性之间存在明确的关联,但单一病原体模型无法清晰描述驱动该疾病分子级联反应的机制,这表明从感染到自身免疫反应的转变可能涉及不止一种病毒。在本综述中,我们探讨了不同神经嗜性病毒之间的相互作用可能在MS中发挥协同致病作用的假设,这一领域目前在文献中研究较少。我们总结了不同病毒在MS发病中作用的矛盾证据,包括疱疹病毒科(人类巨细胞病毒、EB病毒、单纯疱疹病毒HSV-1和HSV-2,以及水痘-带状疱疹病毒)、副粘病毒科(麻疹病毒病毒和风疹病毒)、流感病毒、SARS-CoV-2以及人类内源性逆转录病毒(HERVs)。这些病毒可能通过在共享分子通路中的相互作用、免疫调节以及激活潜伏感染,共同影响MS中特征性的炎症和脱髓鞘过程。本综述评估了病毒相互作用在MS病因中的作用,通过这些病原体的累积和交互效应。现有的流行病学、免疫学和实验数据表明,病毒的共同暴露可能在遗传易感个体中协同影响MS的易感性。明确这些病毒相互作用为识别新型生物标志物和通过靶向控制病毒网络开发预防策略提供了新的研究方向。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述探讨了病毒相互作用在多发性硬化症 (MS) 病因学中的潜在作用,重点关注 麻疹病毒 作为目标实体。核心问题是不同嗜神经病毒之间的相互作用是否会导致多发性硬化症的易感性,特别是在遗传易感个体中。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对病毒相互作用和多发性硬化症文献的综合分析。它包括流行病学、免疫学和实验数据,并评估病毒共同暴露的集体致病作用。该综述没有明确报告荟萃分析或共识过程,但综合了各种研究的结果。
共识与结论
该综述的结论是,病毒共同暴露可能协同作用,影响遗传易感个体的多发性硬化症易感性。它强调了了解这些病原体在多发性硬化症发展背景下的累积和相互作用影响的重要性。
争议与缺口
该综述承认关于不同病毒因子在多发性硬化症中的作用的相互矛盾的证据。它指出,病毒感染与多发性硬化症之间的分子机制尚未完全了解,文献也缺乏对病毒相互作用的集体致病作用的充分研究。
科研与实践启示
该综述表明,表征病毒相互作用为识别新型生物标志物和通过有针对性地控制病毒网络制定预防策略提供了一个新的研究方向。

Multiple Sclerosis and Viruses: « Liaisons Dangereuses ».

Authors: Samanta Plavina, Daniela Malakovska, Jolanta Kalnina, Ilva Trapina, Elina Leonova, Mohammed Habib, Susanne Schifmann, Jegors Paramonovs, Nikolajs Sjakste, Yegor Vassetzky
Original abstract (English)
Multiple sclerosis (MS) is a complex, multifactorial neurodegenerative disease with an aetiology that is still partially elusive. Several risk factors are associated with MS development including genetic predisposition and various environmental stressors; however, recently, viral infections have gained significant attention as a crucial environmental trigger. Despite the well-established link between Epstein-Barr virus (EBV) and MS susceptibility, a single-pathogen model fails to clearly characterise the molecular cascades driving the disease, suggesting that the transition from infection to autoimmune response involves more than one viral agent. In this review we examine the speculation that the interactions among different neurotropic viruses could serve a collective causative role in MS, which is under-researched in the literature at present. We summarise contradictory evidence on the role of different viral agents, including the Herpesviridae family (Human Cytomegalovirus, EBV, Herpes Simplex Viruses HSV-1 and HSV-2, and Varicella Zoster Virus), Paramyxoviridae family (Measles Virus and Rubella Virus), Influenza virus, SARS-CoV-2, and Human Endogenous Retroviruses (HERVs). Through interplay within shared molecular pathways, immune modulation, and activation of latent infections, these viruses may collectively influence the inflammatory and demyelinating processes that characterise MS. This review evaluates the etiological role of viral interplay in MS through the cumulative and interactive effects of these pathogens. The available epidemiological, immunological, and experimental data converge on the concept that viral co-exposures act synergistically to shape MS susceptibility in genetically predisposed individuals. Characterising these viral interactions presents a new research direction for the identification of novel biomarkers and the development of preventative strategies through the targeted control of viral networks.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review explores the potential role of viral interactions in the etiology of multiple sclerosis (MS), with a focus on the measles virus as a target entity. The central question is whether the interplay among different neurotropic viruses contributes to MS susceptibility, particularly in genetically predisposed individuals.
Evidence base and method
The review is a comprehensive analysis of the literature on viral interactions and MS. It includes epidemiological, immunological, and experimental data, and evaluates the collective causative role of viral co-exposures. The review does not explicitly report on meta-analysis or consensus processes, but it synthesizes findings from various studies.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that viral co-exposures may act synergistically to shape MS susceptibility in genetically predisposed individuals. It highlights the importance of understanding the cumulative and interactive effects of these pathogens in the context of MS development.
Controversies and gaps
The review acknowledges contradictory evidence regarding the role of different viral agents in MS. It notes that the molecular mechanisms linking viral infections to MS are not fully understood, and the literature lacks sufficient research on the collective causative role of viral interplay.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that characterising viral interactions presents a new research direction for identifying novel biomarkers and developing preventative strategies through targeted control of viral networks.

宾夕法尼亚州接近 1,400 例病例 麻疹 暴发 - 美国医院协会

Cases near 1,400 in Pennsylvania measles outbreak - American Hospital Association
新闻简报美国医院协会报告称,截至 2026 年 10 月 30 日,宾夕法尼亚州有近 1,400 例 麻疹 暴发 病例。该 麻疹 已得到证实,并强调了重大的公共卫生影响。高风险水平表明病毒有可能进一步传播,尤其是在弱势群体中。虽然该协会正在监测情况,但没有提供有关调查、测试或遏制措施的具体细节。暴发 强调了 疫苗接种 和公共卫生准备的重要性。该报告没有提及任何犬瘟热或风疹等相关病毒的确诊病例,也没有包含有关难以排除的实体的信息。来源状态为完整,表示文章正文已摘录,内容以协会官方声明为准。

美国医院协会报告了 麻疹 暴发 病例接近 1,400 例。该事件是根据暴发数据确认的。
规模、影响与风险
暴发 已导致近 1,400 例病例,表明对公共卫生产生了重大影响。由于进一步传播的可能性和人群的脆弱性,风险级别被明确列为高风险。
应对、状态与不确定性
美国医院协会正在积极报告 暴发,但没有提供有关调查、测试或遏制措施的具体细节。该状态仍在审查中,需要进一步分析。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Cases near 1,400 in Pennsylvania measles outbreak - American Hospital Association

News Brief
The American Hospital Association reported a measles outbreak in Pennsylvania with nearly 1,400 cases as of October 30, 2026. This outbreak is confirmed and highlights a significant public health impact. The high risk level suggests potential for further spread, especially among vulnerable populations. While the association is monitoring the situation, specific details on investigation, testing, or containment measures are not provided. The outbreak underscores the importance of vaccination and public health preparedness. The report does not mention any confirmed cases of related viruses such as canine distemper or rubella, nor does it include information on hard-excluded entities. The source status is full, indicating the original article body was extracted, and the content is based on the association's official statements.

The outbreak has resulted in nearly 1,400 cases, indicating a significant public health impact. The risk level is explicitly stated as high due to the potential for further spread and the vulnerability of the population.
Response, status and uncertainty
The American Hospital Association is actively reporting on the outbreak, but no specific details on investigation, testing, or containment measures are provided. The status remains under review and requires further analysis.
